  - `availability-zone`: The mapping rule for this kind of unit recognises the unit but only passes this setting to a person; it does not decide its value. The availability zone of the subnet. STACKIT networks are regional (stackit_network has no zone field, pinned provider); the zone is chosen per server and volume, and AWS zone names do not map to STACKIT zones. Closes when: A person decides, per source zone, which STACKIT zone (eu01-1, eu01-2 or eu01-3) takes the servers and volumes that sat in it, so that the source's spread and its failure domains are kept.
- **Evidence** (validated against terraform-provider-stackit@0.116.0; terraform-provider-stackit@0.120.0 (re-checked 2026-10-18 against STACKIT provider schema 0.120.0 and the v0.120.0 tag docs: stackit_network re-read, stackit_network attributes unchanged from 0.116.0 (its block description lost the transitional ipv4_nameservers behaviour warning; the attribute description keeps the behaviour); no destroy-protection, DNS64, hostname or per-server record attribute in stackit_network or stackit_server (both unchanged); stackit_public_ip and stackit_dns_record_set unchanged); found compatible through 0.121.0 by the release check): https://github.com/stackitcloud/terraform-provider-stackit/blob/v0.120.0/docs/resources/network.md

### Observability

#### `module.retail_app_ecs.aws_cloudwatch_log_group.ecs_tasks`

- **Source:** `aws_cloudwatch_log_group` — ../../lib/ecs/cluster.tf:10
- **Module:** `module.retail_app_ecs` (../../lib/ecs)
- **Candidate target:** STACKIT Logs (`stackit_logs_instance`, `stackit_logs_access_token`)
- **Target maturity:** GA · lifecycle active · region availability not established by a catalogue snapshot
- **Classification:** RECONFIGURE · **Confidence:** MEDIUM · rule `aws.cloudwatch_log_group/log-group`
- **Feasibility:** CONDITIONAL — blocked by a feature the draft does not carry yet (semantic tc-name); a point the scan could not establish
- **Why:** Log groups become label sets in a STACKIT Logs instance (a label-based Grafana Loki store written over OTLP or the Loki API, retention in days for the whole instance). STACKIT has no log group or stream object: a group's name survives only as a label the shipper sends. Agents switch from the CloudWatch agent to a documented shipper (Grafana Alloy, OpenTelemetry Collector, Vector or Fluent Bit); log groups that keep their logs for the same number of days share one instance.
- **Deployment prerequisites:** passed StackToIT's own draft checks; tofu validate: PASSED; NOT deployment-ready: 1 open decisions, 3 manual actions
  - decision `DEC-OPS-STACKITLOGS-RETENTION-OVER-180-DAYS` (alias `stackitlogs:module.retail_app_ecs.aws_cloudwatch_log_group.ecs_tasks:retention_over_180_days`): Decide whether the logs of module.retail_app_ecs.aws_cloudwatch_log_group.ecs_tasks older than 180 days are kept in an archive outside the Logs instance · the source keeps its logs for ever and a STACKIT Logs instance keeps at most 180 days, so the instance drafted for it (stackit_logs_instance.logs_retention_180d) holds only the last 180 days; logs older than that exist only if a person configures an archive beside it. Choose the archive if the workload needs them; reject it if the requirement is that more than 180 days stay inside STACKIT Logs with no external archive, which STACKIT Logs alone cannot meet
  - manual action `DEC-OPS-STACKITLOGS-ACTION-ARCHIVE-FLOW` (alias `stackitlogs:archive:module.retail_app_ecs.aws_cloudwatch_log_group.ecs_tasks`) (archive flow, before the cutover): Configure a cold-storage log flow for module.retail_app_ecs.aws_cloudwatch_log_group.ecs_tasks: send a copy of its logs, in parallel to the Logs instance stackit_logs_instance.logs_retention_180d, to a long-term store (for example a separate Object Storage bucket) that keeps them for the retention beyond 180 days — the source keeps its logs for ever and a STACKIT Logs instance keeps at most 180 days (retention beyond 180 days is not supported by the managed service): the first 180 days are kept in the Logs instance, and the longer retention is an archive outside it, which STACKIT recommends as a cold-storage approach (a stream of the log data to a low-cost long-term store in parallel to Logs). The plan creates no archive store, configures no stream and sets no retention of the archive. The step exists only when the decision about the logs beyond 180 days chooses the archive; where the requirement is more than 180 days inside STACKIT Logs with no external archive, the archive is rejected and the migration cannot meet that requirement with STACKIT Logs alone
  - manual action `DEC-OPS-STACKITLOGS-ACTION-LABEL-CONVENTION` (alias `stackitlogs:label-convention:module.retail_app_ecs.aws_cloudwatch_log_group.ecs_tasks`) (label convention, before the cutover): Choose a static label and send the name of module.retail_app_ecs.aws_cloudwatch_log_group.ecs_tasks (and of its streams) from its log producers as the value of that label — STACKIT Logs has no log group or stream object that can hold a name, only labels the shipper sends (no-log-group-or-stream-object); the plan chooses no label key and reconfigures no producer
  - manual action `DEC-OPS-STACKITLOGS-ACTION-TARGET-PRECONDITION` (alias `stackitlogs:token:stackit_logs_instance.logs_retention_180d`) (target precondition, before the cutover): Before any log source writes to stackit_logs_instance.logs_retention_180d: create a write access token for the instance (stackit_logs_access_token with the permission write), and point the shipper at the instance's ingest_url (Loki API) or ingest_otlp_url (OpenTelemetry) from an address var.logs_acl_retention_180d admits — the plan generates no access token: the token is a credential written to the state, its lifetime (unset, it never expires) and its permissions are choices the source does not state, and one token belongs to one instance; the access list is var.logs_acl_retention_180d, a required input with no default the person gives before the instance is planned: what an instance with no ACL entry allows is not established, so the plan neither leaves it out nor accepts an empty list
- **WARNING** (source-provider-unpinned): No lock file entry pins aws (>= 3.0, >= 3.29, >= 4.18, >= 4.30, >= 4.59, >= 5.64, >= 5.79, ~> 5.0): the version the plan would use is not proven, so the knowledge base (checked against 5.100.0) is not taken as proof of it (stated once for the whole estate, with the units it covers and the rest of the words)
- **Critical semantics left to you to decide or accept (1):** tc-name
- **Open plan decision(s):** `DEC-OPS-STACKITLOGS-RETENTION-OVER-180-DAYS` (alias `stackitlogs:module.retail_app_ecs.aws_cloudwatch_log_group.ecs_tasks:retention_over_180_days`) — a person decides, and until then the unit is **not automation-eligible**.
- **Manual action(s) required before apply:** `DEC-OPS-STACKITLOGS-ACTION-ARCHIVE-FLOW` (alias `stackitlogs:archive:module.retail_app_ecs.aws_cloudwatch_log_group.ecs_tasks`), `DEC-OPS-STACKITLOGS-ACTION-LABEL-CONVENTION` (alias `stackitlogs:label-convention:module.retail_app_ecs.aws_cloudwatch_log_group.ecs_tasks`), `DEC-OPS-STACKITLOGS-ACTION-TARGET-PRECONDITION` (alias `stackitlogs:token:stackit_logs_instance.logs_retention_180d`) — a person does them, and until then the unit is **not automation-eligible**.
- **Property accounting:** 1 of 1 meaningful properties accounted
- **1 critical setting(s) left to you** — **not automation-eligible**:
  - workload compatibility (1; REVIEW 1): `name` — plan: stackit_logs_instance.logs_retention_180d name of module.retail_app_ecs.aws_cloudwatch_log_group.ecs_tasks MANUAL_ACTION (`DEC-OPS-STACKITLOGS-ACTION-LABEL-CONVENTION` (alias `stackitlogs:label-convention:module.retail_app_ecs.aws_cloudwatch_log_group.ecs_tasks`)) (contract aws.cloudwatch_log_group/tc-name:REVIEW)
- **Why those are open, and what closes them:**
  - `tc-name`: The mapping rule for this kind of unit recognises the unit but only passes this setting to a person; it does not decide its value. aws_cloudwatch_log_group.name is the address a log producer writes to (awslogs-group, PutLogEvents logGroupName). The name is therefore not carried by anything the draft generates, and no producer is rewired: a person decides the label convention and reconfigures each producer - review (the plan holds it as a LABEL_CONVENTION manual action). Held critical: the workload, its clients or its neighbours rely on it (an address, a name, a protocol or an execution contract). Closes when: A person decides the label convention under which the log group name survives in STACKIT Logs and reconfigures each producer to send it; the draft configures no shipper.
